# CHAPTER I.

## On the Metallic Tincture.

Because so much is said and written about the Metallic Tincture <term>Tincturâ Metallicâ</term> <note>A "tincture" in alchemy refers to a potent substance—often the Philosopher's Stone itself—capable of "staining" or transforming the inner nature of a base metal into gold or silver.</note> (commonly called the Philosopher's Stone), and yet few of those who toil so bitterly <note>original: *blutsauer*. Literally "blood-sour," an idiom meaning to work oneself to the point of exhaustion or extreme hardship.</note> to work it out and gain possession of it actually know what the Metallic Tincture or Philosopher’s Stone is, or how it is possible that a small quantity of it can transform a large lump of lead <note>original symbol: ♄. In the astrological and alchemical systems, the planet Saturn represents the metal lead.</note> and other metals into pure silver or gold; I have, out of love for those who exert themselves greatly in this art, composed the following and shared it with them, so that they might have the pleasure of knowing truly and without the slightest doubt what such a tincture is, what it consists of, and how it must function and serve for nothing other than the ripening <term>Reiffung</term> <note>Alchemists believed that metals were "living" things that grew in the earth; "base" metals like lead were considered "unripe" versions of gold.</note> of base ores. Many people have indeed written extensively about it, but so strangely foolishly and with so many fables, that I myself—who, praise be to God! know from experience what they intend to describe—cannot rightly say who speaks the truth and who lies, except for four or five authors whom I suspect, based on many circumstances, to have been possessors of this holy masterpiece. Otherwise, the rest write so generally and use so many lions <note>The "Lion" was a frequent and confusing alchemical symbol; for example, the "Green Lion" might represent vitriol or antimony, while the "Red Lion" often represented the final stage of the Stone.</note>

<summary>In this opening chapter, the author defines the Metallic Tincture (Philosopher's Stone) and its ability to transmute lead into precious metals. He criticizes the obscure and "foolish" writings of most alchemists, promising to provide a clearer explanation for dedicated practitioners.</summary>

<keywords>Metallic Tincture, Philosopher's Stone, transmutation, lead, gold, silver, ripening of ores, alchemical symbols, lions</keywords>

...of Lions, Snakes, Eagles, Dragons, Foxes, Fish, Birds of Hermes <note>A "Bird of Hermes" is a common alchemical symbol representing volatile substances—those that evaporate or "fly" easily—or the process of sublimation.</note>, Grey Wolves, old and young men, Danes <note>Likely referring to the people of Denmark; alchemical fables often used various nationalities to represent different stages or "families" of metallic evolution.</note> and beautiful virgins, Gods and Goddesses, Planets, Dragons’ Heads and Tails <note>In astronomy, these are the lunar nodes; in alchemy, they often represent the beginning and end of a specific chemical process or the movement of spirits.</note>, Metals, Blood and Sweat, Urine of the Moon and Excrements of the Sun—because such ways of speaking can be applied to so many different things, I do not rightly understand them, nor can anyone explain the truth and actual meaning of them to my satisfaction <note>original: *auf mein Gewissen*. Literally "upon my conscience," implying a deep, personal certainty.</note>.

Therefore, I will here name the causes by their common names, so that every peasant who is familiar with the mines <note>The author values the practical, hands-on knowledge of miners over the abstract theories of scholars.</note> can understand me. This is especially important as I have grasped this secret of nature not from books or writings, but through God and the guidance of a single man. Thus, I am not bound to follow the book-writers in their so-called <term>Philosophical Terms</term> <note>original: *Philosophischen Terminis*. The technical jargon and specialized vocabulary used by academic alchemists.</note>. Common liquid mercury.</note> is born from it; for this is the main point. To my knowledge, as long as the world has stood, no more than two people—masters of this art—have written honestly and sincerely about the true nature of this Universal Mercury before it becomes a metallic water. The few others, though they might provide some instruction, only begin their teaching with the metallic quicksilver and its...

### Chapter 1.

...ore, namely when the <term>Universal Matter</term> <note>original: *Universal-Materia*. The primordial substance from which all things in the universe were thought to be composed.</note> has already been processed and <term>determined</term> <note>original: *determiniret*. Directed toward a specific form or purpose.</note> to such an extent that it exists in a metallic state, appearing in the form of a white <term>ore</term> <note>original: *Mineræ*.</note> or the liquid metal (which the common man calls <term>Mercury</term> <note>original: *Mercurius*. Common quicksilver.</note>).

From this, no student could become wise or find the true matter of the Metallic <term>Tincture</term> <note>A transformative substance, often equated with the Philosopher's Stone, capable of "dyeing" or changing base metals into gold.</note>. Instead, they tormented themselves without success by working with common mercury or other mineral things that contain some metallic quality. The general terms used by authors led many to believe that because the <term>Mercury of the Philosophers</term> <note>original: *Philosophorum Mercurius*. A refined, spiritualized version of mercury distinct from the common metal.</note> was supposed to be universal, it must not be the common kind, but rather must come from the <term>salt of the earth</term> <note>original: *Saltz der Erden*. Likely referring to nitre or vitriol.</note>, or a type of clay or loam. They believed this earth could act like a magnet to draw the general salt of the air into itself and unite it with its own; from this, a metallic essence or even a metal itself would arise, which would necessarily have to **dissolve**, **decompose** <note>original: *putrificiren*. The alchemical stage of "putrefaction" or rotting, seen as necessary for rebirth.</note>, and (to use common speech) **solidify** <note>original: *figiren*. To make a "volatile" or evaporating substance stable under heat.</note> the gold once more.

Others sought the Mercury of the Wise in **animal** and **vegetable substances**. However, because these could not be brought into a metallic nature, they did not fundamentally penetrate the <term>Gold</term> <note>original: *☉* (the astrological symbol for the Sun, used here to represent gold).</note>—even if they were diligently mixed and <term>digested</term> <note>original: *digerirt*. A process of gentle, continuous heating.</note>—and they left its innermost essence unchanged. Some, and indeed the cleverest ones, thought that because all **sublunary** <note>original: *Sublunarische*. Literally "under the moon"; referring to the earthly realm of change and decay as opposed to the eternal heavens.</note>

## On the Metallic Tincture <note>original: *De Tinctura Metallica*.</note>

Just as all creatures derive their life, growth, and sustenance from the air—and even the metals themselves originate from it and, as long as they remain attached to their roots and live, "breathe" through the weather, as it were—so must the **Mercury of the wise** <term>Mercurius</term> <note>original: *weisen Mercurius*. This refers not to common quicksilver, but to a refined, spiritualized essence considered the "soul" of metals.</note> be taken from the air before it specifies itself into any single earthly thing. However, because these well-meaning people used false magnets to catch such a **Mercury** or spirit, their work did not succeed, and they obtained only a **saltpeter** <term>Nitrum</term> <note>Commonly known as nitre or saltpeter; many alchemists believed this salt contained the "spirit of the world" captured from the atmosphere.</note> through their magnet. Who could have imagined that the true, living **magnet** for the **universal mercury** <term>universal ☿</term> <note>The symbol ☿ represents Mercury. The "universal mercury" is the primordial liquid from which all metals were thought to grow.</note> would come from a **living human being**?

Truly, almost all clever workers and **chemists** <term>Chymici</term> <note>Practitioners of early modern chemistry and alchemy.</note> have been offended by this when they happened to hear or read of it. They considered this great wisdom to be foolishness because it seemed impossible to them that a metal could be born from a human being, though it is the plain truth. No thing in the whole world, except for the **human magnet** <term>Magnet</term> <note>The author is suggesting that the human body or a substance derived from it acts as the primary tool to attract and condense the spirit of the air into a physical form.</note>, can make our air visible and deliver it to the masterpiece of the **metallic nature** <term>Metallen-Natur</term>. Only recently, my most trusted correspondent and dear friend assured me in a letter that a good friend had brought forth not only the **Philosophers' Mercury** <term>Mercurium philosoph.</term> but also another **Gold** <term>☉</term> <note>The symbol ☉ represents the Sun or Gold.</note> from our **universal solvent** <term>Menstruo universali</term> <note>original: *Menstruo universali*. In alchemy, a "menstruum" is a powerful solvent; the universal version was thought to be able to reduce any matter to its original state.</note> and **primordial matter** <term>Materia chaotica</term> <note>original: *Materia chaotica*. The "chaotic" or unformed matter that exists before it is shaped into a specific substance like gold or lead.</note> to the greatest astonishment. So that this instruction of mine may proceed in an orderly fashion and be better understood, I will divide it into chapters—

<summary>The author argues that the "Mercury of the Wise" is extracted from the air using the human being as a "living magnet," a controversial claim that many contemporary chemists rejected as impossible. He cites a correspondent's success in producing gold and philosophical mercury from this "universal solvent" as proof of his theory.</summary>

<keywords>Mercury of the Wise, air as source of metals, human magnet, saltpeter, Chemists, Philosophers' Mercury, Gold, Universal Solvent, Primordial Matter, bio-alchemy</keywords>

# Chapter I. On the Metallic Tincture. <note>original: *Caput I. De Tinctura Metallica.*</note>

...divide, and although at the beginning of my writing I intended not to say much about the Universal Matter <term>Universal-Materia</term> <note>The primordial substance from which all things in the universe were thought to originate.</note>, but only to touch upon it briefly and—as mentioned above—to explain only what the Recipe <term>℞</term> <note>The traditional symbol for a medical or alchemical prescription; here it refers to the specific formula for the Great Work.</note> and the so-called Philosophers' Stone <term>Stein der Weisen</term> actually are, my conscience, my love for my neighbor, and my concern for the talent I received from God drive me otherwise. This talent, which I possess as a member of Christ’s body, I must not return to my Lord and God on the Day of Judgment without having put it to sufficient use <note>A reference to the biblical Parable of the Talents (Matthew 25), where servants are judged on how they invested what was entrusted to them.</note>. Therefore, I cannot remain silent about what is necessary for the discovery of the holy truth and for leading my fellow Christians away from the path of error.

The time of revelation has come, and the Lord does not wish to keep any treasures hidden from mankind. As an unworthy instrument of God, I write clearly and distinctly about the primordial essence of creation <term>Geschöpff-Urwesen</term> and its origin; and whatever might have been omitted will not be denied by Heaven to those who have understood the rest and put it to good use. I go the way of all the world and daily await my death in faith in Jesus Christ. His grace is so powerful in me—an unworthy man—toward the knowledge of God and His creatures, that He will not deny me the eternal, imperishable treasures. ## CHAPTER II.

### On Nature and Motion, etc.

BEcause these words occur so frequently in books, I have thought it well—according to the grace I have received and as I have found through experience in the innermost foundation of created things <term>Geschöpffe</term>—to briefly report on them and explain them. This is so that these ways of speaking may also be understood and no longer give cause for darkness and error due to their obscurity. 

Therefore, Nature is not what is commonly described—a quantity <term>Quantum</term> <note>The author is distinguishing between "Nature" as a measurable physical amount (quantity) and "Nature" as an inherent quality or energetic state.</note>—nor is it the cause of a quantity or essence. Rather, it is a quality or property through which every essence manifests itself. And although an essence may manifest as bitter yesterday and sweet today—which is possible and can be demonstrated through practical works—one nevertheless recognizes in the knowledge of the quantity <term>Quantum</term> or the essence a sameness; one sees that it has merely altered itself through a different motion <term>Bewegung</term> and opened itself into a different quality or nature. 

It is not that by this opinion I despise the knowledge of nature or of qualities, or wish to hold them as useless; rather, it is solely so that the person who hungers for wisdom <term>Weißheit</term> may not linger long in vain upon the...

### On Nature and Motion, etc.

[...or] to seek Nature as an essence <term>Wesen</term>, and in such a quality <term>Quale</term> <note>Latin: *quale*. This refers to the "what-ness" or the essential character of a substance.</note> without grasping the quantity <term>Quantum</term> <note>Latin: *quantum*. This refers to the measurable magnitude or physical amount.</note>, one remains entangled and confused in one’s thoughts and labor. And although no quantity can be grasped or understood unless its quality, nature, or kind is rightly perceived, nevertheless no one can bring this science of quality to right use—to a certain art or desired work, without fear of deceiving oneself and failing—unless the quantity is certainly and well known to him; for **no quality exists without quantity.** <note>original: *nulla qualitas est sine quantitate*.</note>

But so that the words "Nature" and "Motion" may be better understood hereafter, I describe Nature as being: the **internal motion of things** <note>original: *Motus rerum internus*.</note>, the inner movement of those objects. And Motion: **the presence of the sustaining divine Word.** <note>original: *Verbi divini conservantis præsentia*.</note>

Upon and from this Word hangs the universal Light; upon and from the Light, the universal Spirit; upon and from the Spirit, the universal Salt <term>Saltz</term> <note>In the "Tria Prima" of alchemy (Salt, Sulfur, Mercury), Salt represents the principle of fixity, body, and the physical container for spiritual forces.</note>. Whoever now, through God's grace, recognizes Light, Spirit, and Salt, and understands the ray of Light, the number of the Spirit, and the weight of the Salt within all creatures, he can truly look into the depths of Nature—that is, into motion itself—and rejoice in the wondrous presence of the Word with benefit and in actual deed.

# CHAPTER III.

## On Light.

It is necessary—so that nothing may be omitted which serves for the sufficient explanation of the truth and the instruction of my neighbor—to write how I understand the word "Light," and what Light actually is. Regarding this matter, many treatises have indeed been written by subtle people which one may read, wherein it is disputed whether Light is an essence <term>Wesen</term> <note>In this context, the author refers to the debate over whether light is a physical substance or an abstract property.</note> or not, and various opinions are presented. I, however, do not wish to borrow anything from them nor to refute anyone, but simply to reveal my own knowledge of it.

Light proceeded from God, and in the beginning of Creation—before anything else existed except the glorious World-Slime <term>Welt-Schleim</term> <note>Also known as *limus mundi*, this refers to the primordial "chaos" or raw, unformed matter of the universe before God organized it.</note>—it was created out of the divine Breath. It was intended to be a subject <term>Subjectum</term> <note>original Latin: *Subjectum*. Here meaning the underlying foundation or substance.</note> or vehicle <term>Vehiculum</term> <note>original Latin: *Vehiculum*. A carrier or medium through which a higher force acts.</note> of the eternal Word or divine Breath, with which and within which the Lord radiated into the World-Slime and began the work of Creation.

The matter of this world had lost the light through the fall of the Devil, its former king. Through this light, the Lord—acting within Lucifer before his fall—had radiated through the matter or the glorious kingdom of Lucifer, bringing it into effect and maintaining it in a state of rarefaction <note>The author suggests that before the fall, matter was "thinner," more spiritual, and luminous, rather than heavy and dense.</note>. But after the fall, it was nothing but darkness, even into the abyss.

## CHAPTER IV.
### On the Universal Spirit of the World.

The word **Spirit** <note>original: *Geist*</note> is understood in various ways, and many different opinions regarding its explanation can be found. # CHAPTER V.

# On the Element.

The word "Element" is Latin <note>original: *Elementum*</note> / and signifies the beginning of a thing / just as the letters of languages are called *elementa* <note>elements</note> / because words are composed from them; among philosophers, it signifies a **primordial essence** <term>primordial essence</term> <note>original: *Uhrwesen*. In this context, it refers to a fundamental, original substance from which all material things are derived.</note> of things / and commonly four such primordial essences are counted / namely **Fire** (△), **Air** (🜁), **Water** (🜄), and **Earth** (🜃) <note>The original text uses the standard alchemical symbols for the four elements.</note> / which opinion / that they are incorrect / and not understood from the foundation of the **knowledge of Creation** <term>knowledge of Creation</term> <note>original: *Schöpffungs-Kundigung*. Also in the *Letter of Response to W. Fr. J.* / and therefore it is not necessary to repeat it here; I will mention only this / for the sake of those / to whom perhaps the Latin language is unknown / or who do not have the aforementioned booklets at hand / that there is only one visible and tangible element / namely the slimy water / or the watery slime: This is the first visible beginning of all things / and within it lie hidden Light, Spirit, and Salt / all things are also brought back and transformed into the same in their final and ultimate dissolution / as soon as / namely / a greater or heavier weight of the universal Spirit, Salt, or **Mercury** <term>Mercury</term> <note>The author refers to the alchemical principle of *Mercurius*, the spiritual "glue" or life-force of matter, rather than the liquid metal quicksilver.</note> [is applied] by the **artist** <term>artist</term> <note>A common term for a practicing alchemist or chemist.</note> / or without the assistance of human hands / through the universal air-

<header>Chapter VI. Of the Air.</header>

When the common Salt-Spirit <note>original: "Salz-Geistes"; refers to the volatile, acidic, or "spiritual" essence of salt</note> with the general Salt-Body <note>indicated by the symbol ⊖</note> is changed—whether through external accidents or the internal movement of the astral flow <note>original: "Gestirn-Flusse"; the alchemical belief that spiritual influences constantly stream down from the stars</note> or otherwise—the flow of air dissolves the Salt-Spirit of that body. Or rather, in its passage, the air leaches out the Salt-Spirit, just as water leaches a soluble Salt-Body <note>original: "Salz-Leib"</note> out of the earth. When this happens, a thing falls apart and becomes powerless—except for the fact that a Niter <note>original: "Nitrum"; here referring to a vital mineral salt or saltpeter believed to hold the "seed" of life</note> can still be formed from the Salt-Body that remained behind.

What I have just mentioned is not known to the learned <note>original: "Literatis"; referring to university-educated scholars or academics</note>, and it is therefore quite necessary that it be explained at greater length to provide a better understanding of it. I therefore direct the reader to the chapter on Mercury <note>original: "Mercurio"</note> and Vitriol <note>original: "Vitriolo"</note>, where he shall find a more complete explanation.

The Air gives birth to the Magnet, but the Magnet gives birth to and makes our Air visible. Here, I have surely revealed the true truth to you. Pray to **God** that He may bless your beginning.)

# CHAPTER VII.
## Of Water.

Water is also counted as an element by the learned scholars <note>original: "Literatis"; referring to university-educated academics</note>. Its definition can be found in the works of many authors. I find it to be the most pliable <note>original: "zweigsten"; literally "twig-like," here meaning flexible or adaptable in form</note> or most liquid part of the World Slime <note>original: "Welt Schleims"; a term for the *materia prima* or the primordial, chaotic substance from which the universe was organized</note> or elements, and a vehicle for the air. It gives all things (when mixed with a little earth) their body; just as their soul arises from the air and their spirit from the Air-Spirit <note>original: "Lufft-Geist"</note>. It easily becomes earth if it only finds a suitable matter <note>original: "convenientem materiam"</note>.

To be sure, it can also be quickly transformed into earth through mere artificial motion; however, in this way, which acts rapidly, it becomes powerless and loses too much of the Spirit-Salt <note>original: "Geist-Saltz"; the volatile, life-giving essence</note>, because the most subtle particles—when the coarser ones must quickly thicken and settle together—are licked away and leached out by the air that is always passing through. For this reason, common thin water is also not suitable <note>original: "gülden"; literally "golden," meaning ideal or effective</note> for the Philosopher's Stone <note>original: "Stein der Weisen"</note>, and cannot be its starting material <note>original: "Materia"</note>, because the practitioner cannot transform it into earth except through a rapid and violent motion; whereby, as has been said, the Mercury <note>original: "Mercurius"</note> necessary for the goal is lost.

Indeed, water also becomes earth without violent motion, and without [losing] its...

By the term <term>World-Slime</term> <note>original: "Welt-Schleim"; a concept similar to the "primordial soup" or the first chaotic matter of creation</note> (I sometimes call the World-Slime "corrupted" because the <term>matter</term> <note>original: "Materia"</note> from which it sprouted became lowly and turned to slime through <term>Lucifer’s Fall</term>; at other times I call it "noble," because the <term>Ideal form</term> <note>original: "Idea"</note> of this entire world lay hidden within it. ...describe it as our matter.

Although our <term>World-Slime</term> <note>original: "Welt-Schleim"; the primordial substance or "first matter" (Materia Prima) from which all things are created</note> is all things at once—Air, Water, and Earth—it is nevertheless good for our masterpiece, and specifically because of its <term>Salt</term>, which comes from the Air and from Mankind, and consists of <term>Mercury</term> <note>original: "Mercurio"</note> and <term>Vitriol</term> <note>original: "Vitriolo"</note>. Vitriol, however, has a contracting power, originating from the first fall of the <term>Primordial Being</term> <note>original: "Uhrwesen"; the original, uncorrupted state of divinity or existence</note>, namely the Devil, when the Light became Darkness. This <term>astringent</term> <note>original: "adstringirende"; binding or tightening</note> quality, which was highly harmful to the Primordial Being before the Creation and drew everything together and thickened it more and more, is—following the creation of the <term>New Light</term>, which rules through the Spirit in the Salt or Mercury and is thinning and dividing—highly necessary for the current world and its workings. 

Through this, when the Air has captured its Salt-Spirit or Mercury, all creatures come forth, grow, and are sustained. Without Mercury and Vitriol, and without the right, orderly proportion of both, nothing can come to be or persist; because Mercury thins everything, while Vitriol thickens everything. According to their proportion, a thing shall be thin or thick, hard or soft, <term>active</term> <note>original: "fleissig"; in this context, it refers to the volatility or the "flow" of a substance</note> or stable;

# Chapter IX.

Indeed, according to how much Air <term>🜁</term> or Water <term>🜄</term> joins itself to each body during movement, and also as far as the form and power of the **Womb** <note>original: "Matrix"; in alchemy, the "matrix" is the formative environment or vessel—often compared to a biological womb—where the elements react and grow</note> will permit, it creates a cordial harmony and allows these two workers, despite their unequal properties, to become united. **Mercury** <note>original: "Mercurius"</note> maintains the spirit, soul, and life through its constant thinning; **Vitriol** <note>original: "Vitriolum"</note> maintains the body and the **Womb** through its constant thickening. As soon as the proper **proportion** <note>original: "proportion"</note> is changed—whether by the craftsman or by an external accident—a being is forced out of its previous shape. If there is too much **Mercury**, the substance is thinned, swollen, **dissolved** <note>original: "solviret"</note>, **fermented** <note>original: "fermentiret"</note>, and even led to rot and final ruin. If, however, the **weight** <note>original: "pondus"</note> of the **Vitriol** is increased, then a thin, liquid, loose, porous, and swollen thing contracts; it becomes thick, solid, and hard, sinks down, and is transformed into stone and metal.

The origin of our **World-Slime** is (Note Well) from Mankind and the Air; for this reason, the poor man has it more and better than the King. For this reason, it is nowhere to be bought, because it would be worth nothing and can be used too little <note>in the sense of being too common for commercial trade</note>; thus, it is to be found everywhere, because Man carries the **Matter of the Magnet** <note>original: "Materia Magnetis"; a substance believed to have an attractive power to "pull" spiritual forces out of the air</note> with him everywhere. With this—by means of slight hand-movements and the proper instruments—he can, if he wishes, catch the universal spirit of the air and turn it into **Salt** <term>🜔</term>.

Our **Matter** <note>original: "Materia"</note> is found on the highest mountains: For when man works with his magnet on the highest mountain, his hand—in which the magnet lies—along with the **Instrument** <note>original: "Instrumento"</note> into which the spiritual salt enters, is even higher than the mountain and higher than the Highest.

The **Matter of the Universal Magnet** <note>original: "Materia Magnetis universalis"</note> is thrown out of the pharmacies and spice-shops, poured onto the dung-heap by the maidservants, found on the public streets, and often serves children for their play; in its raw form, it is both a good medicine and a wicked poison. Subsequently, according to the testimony of Holy Scripture and the knowledge of both Being and Non-being—if it is only shown to him—it is the best, most glorious, and most necessary thing for mankind's soul; but at the same time, it is also the worst, most contemptible, and most superfluous substance among all that man has, possesses, or casts off from himself in the world. Our matter is **animal** <note>original: "animalisch"</note>, because it comes from man and brings forth **insects** <note>original: "Insecta"</note> from itself; it is **mineral** <note>original: "Mineralisch"</note>, because from it and the air—without the further addition of any other thing—**metals** <note>original: "Metalla"</note> are born; and it is **vegetable** <note>original: "Vegetabilisch"</note>, because not only do **vegetables** <note>original: "Vegetabilia"</note> grow from it, but also because from its magnet a salt **pre-** <note>This word is completed on the following page as "prepared"</note>

<header>Chapter XI. On My *Teſſâ*.</header>

# CHAPTER XI.
## On My *Teſſâ*.

*Teſſâ* is a new technical term, and contains within itself a special secret in <term>creation-lore</term> <note>German: *Geſchöpff-Kunde*; the study of created things or natural science</note>. I have chosen it because I could not name or express what I mean by it with any other known <term>term</term> <note>original: *Termino*</note>. Although it has been used in various ways in my other writings, and not always intended with the same meaning, this was done solely to "place a bolt" before the Art to exclude the unworthy, so that they might not grasp the matters so directly. Besides this single word, I have otherwise, to my knowledge, used no terms that were not intended in their proper and well-known sense.

I will now, however, openly explain and reveal it: that it means nothing other than the **snow-white earth**, without smell or taste, which has been made from the <term>world-slime</term> <note>German: *Welt-Schleim*; likely referring to the *Limus Mundi* or the primordial chaotic matter from which the world was formed</note> without the use of fire. Within it, the universal <term>mercury</term> <note>original: *Mercurius*</note> and <term>vitriol</term> <note>original: *Vitriolum*</note> lie hidden, as can be seen in detail in the 8th Chapter concerning the Earth.

(Here Aletophilus <note>A pseudonym meaning "Lover of Truth," likely the editor or a commentator</note>, to testify to the truth before all the world, cannot leave it unmentioned that he has seen such *Teſſâ* with his own eyes, held it in his hands, and received some of it. Yes, and he also afterwards, in the <term>elaboration</term> <note>original: *Elaboration*; the laboratory process of refining the substance</note>, as far

## CHAPTER XII.
### On Salt.

This chapter was omitted by Baron Otto Helwig’s beloved brother, Mr. Licentiate Christoph Helwig—my most heart-trusted friend and correspondent. The reason is perhaps because his brother mixed in the "Fall of Lucifer" <note>Referring to the biblical narrative of the rebellion of angels</note> according to a personal whim and imagination, arranging it according to this **Mystery of Nature** <note>original: *Mysterio Naturæ*</note>. Because such a thing would not sit well with our **theologians** <note>original: *Theologis*</note>, nor can it be demonstrated or deduced from Holy Scripture in such a manner, the best advice—as I have already reported before—is that one should leave the **mysteries** <note>original: *mysteria*</note> and **Articles of Faith** <note>original: *Articulos fidei*</note> unmixed with this **Mystery of Nature**. 

For although it might serve as a form of **illustration** <note>An explanatory example</note>, one must not attempt to use it **in place of a demonstrative principle** <note>original: *loco Principii demonstrativi*; meaning as a fundamental proof</note>, which Holy Scripture alone is. Such things have commonly been done by various philosophers and artists—especially **Wasserstein** <note>Referring to the author of 'The Water-Stone of the Wise' (*Wasserstein der Weisen*), a famous alchemical text</note>, **Boehme** <note>Jakob Boehme (1575–1624), a German Christian mystic</note>, **Theophrastus** <note>Paracelsus (Philippus Aureolus Theophrastus Bombastus von Hohenheim)</note>, and others. They have even drawn and interpreted countless **sayings of Scripture** <note>original: *Dicta Scripturæ*</note> toward this subject, especially the Song of Solomon. We do not **approve** <note>original: *approbiren*</note> of this at all, but only here

<page-num>171</page-num>
<header>On Salt.</header>
<sig>iv</sig>
<meta>catchword: I</meta>

[I shall] begin here with the **Philosophical Mystery** <note>original: *Mysterio Philosophico*</note> in and of itself. And thus I shall include that which serves the matter from the **Author's Manuscript** <note>original: *Manuscripto des Autoris*</note>.

Salt is a good thing, the most magnificent among all visible beings, the seed and grain of the entire world; and indeed of two kinds: fluid and constant, thinning and thickening, sweet and sour. Antiquity calls the subtle, volatile salt by many names, but principally **Mercury** <note>original: *Mercurium*</note>, though I call it spirit-salt, **universal, volatile, and astral salt** <note>original: *Sal universale, volatile & astrale*</note>. From these two salts many others arise, due to the common and distinct union of **Mercury** and **Vitriol** <note>original: *Vitrioli*; here referring to the fixed, acidic principle of salts</note> (the upper- and lower-fluid salt, or air- and earth-salt), which two **salts** <note>original: *Salia*</note>, namely common cooking salt and **nitre** <note>original: *Nitrum*; saltpeter</note>, are the most prominent, which also contain within them a great **Vitriol** and **Mercury**.

(**Note** <note>original: *NB.*</note>: Although there is less **vitriol** than **mercury** in our matter or **world-slime** <note>original: *Welt-Schleim*; a term for the *Prima Materia* or primordial substance from which the world was formed</note>, yet in **distillation** <note>original: *destillatione*</note> a common cooking salt is formed from it in the **dead head** <note>original: *Capite mortuo*; the chemical residue left in a vessel after all volatile components have been driven off</note>, because the white **mercury** of the slime passes over the **retort** <note>original: *Retorte*; a glass vessel with a long downward-pointing neck used for distilling substances</note> in the water, **saline spirit, and oil** <note>original: *Spiritu Salino & Oleo*</note>; so that the small amount of **mercury** remaining allows the **weight of vitriol** <note>original: *pondus Vitrioli*</note> to prevail over it, from which a common cooking salt is formed. How much **mercury** and **vitriol** must be together, I have not been able to **examine** <note>original: *examiniren*</note> precisely until now due to many hindrances.)

<summary>This page defines Salt as the fundamental "seed" of the world, categorizing it into volatile (Mercury) and fixed (Vitriol) forms. It also describes a laboratory observation where distillation separates these principles, leaving behind common salt in the residue.</summary>

<page-num>172</page-num>
<header>Chapter XII.</header>

I have written elsewhere that our salt in the air is no salt, but only a spirit, and only becomes salt in the water. God works with three things, which are from one and dwell in one; the three things from which all beings arise are Light, Spirit, and Salt, which dwell in the air; with these, Water, Vitriol <note>original: *Vitriol*; here referring to the acidic or mineral principle of salts</note>, and Earth are made, which are also from one and are brought back into one.

Sea-salt, because of its quantity and the coarseness of its vitriol, preserves <note>original: *conserviret*</note> all things salted with it from fermentation <note>original: *fermentation*</note> and putrefaction <note>original: *putrefaction*; decay</note>, and for a long time binds together their spirit-salt or volatile salt, so that it cannot be, as it were, licked up or robbed by the world-spirit in the flow of air, until finally, after several years, through the constant action <note>original: *action*</note> of the spirit-salt of vitriol in common cooking salt <note>original: *Sale culinari*</note>, it gradually loses its power, and from both—due to the quantity of the present spirit—it becomes the salt.

But as concerns our world-slime <term>world-slime: original *Welt-Schleim*, a term for the *Prima Materia* or primordial substance from which the world was formed</term>, when it expands into air, water, and earth, some of the finest parts of the slime-salt remain with the air through the action of the volatile spirit-salt, and are drawn off in the air from the water and earth at the same time; its coarse parts, however, remain in the water and earth as a contracting salt. The upper part the ancients called Mercury <note>original: *Mercurium*; the volatile, fluid principle</note>, because they made their Mercury for the metallic masterpiece <note>The "Great Work" or creation of the Philosopher's Stone</note> from it; the lower part [they called] vitriol, or

<meta>catchword: Vi-</meta>

<summary>This page describes the alchemical trinity of Light, Spirit, and Salt as the foundations of all matter, explaining how salt acts as a preservative by binding volatile spirits. It identifies "world-slime" as the source of both Mercury (the volatile upper part) and Vitriol (the fixed lower part).</summary>

<page-num>173</page-num>
<header>Of Salt.</header>

**Conquering Oil** <note>original: *Victrix oleum*; a term used for a powerful alchemical solvent believed to "conquer" or fix volatile spirits</note>, because with it the universal spirit is not only captured, made visible, and turned into salt, but also because its dissolving, partitioning, and thinning nature is so overcome by it that it must become stable in the **Vitriol** <term>Vitriol: a term for various sulfate minerals, highly valued in alchemy for their acidic and transformative properties</term> and allow itself to be enclosed in the body of the air and the water. <term>oo</term> <note>original: *oo*; a common alchemical shorthand for *oleum* or oil</note> it is called because one uses it only in the form of a fatty liquid <note>original: *in forma pinguis liquoris*</note> (as one also says **Oil** of Vitriol, **Oil** of Salt <note>represented by the symbol 🜔</note>, **Oil** of Copper <note>represented by the symbol ♀</note>, of Niter, etc., which are nonetheless all merely salty liquids); and our Vitriol in a dry form <note>original: *in forma sicca*</note> does not accept the spirit of light, but only in the form of a fatty liquid. For when it is applied to the air in a wonderful warm instrument <note>likely referring to a specialized alchemical vessel or furnace</note>, the universal spirit sinks into it in great quantities and is for the most part turned into salt. It is thus truly, for itself and the artist, a rich **Conquering Oil**, or a **Conquering Liquid**, because without this, nothing could be accomplished in self-motion and the Art <note>the "Art" refers to the practice of Alchemy</note>.

The spirit-salt is also called the **Astral**, and the body-salt the **Central**, and its essence is nicely expressed through these names. Both salts are easily turned into one salt—like kitchen salt—through Art in the fire, and in that same state of rigor they perform a great effect; they also deliver from such a kitchen-salt-body true **mercury-metal** <note>original: *Queck-Metall*; likely a purified or "living" form of mercury</note>, without the addition of any mineral thing. This mercury-metal, however, is not as good as that which has been made without fire.

## CHAPTER XIII.
# On Mercury, Vitriol, and Sulfur.

**Mercury** generally signifies the liquid metal called **quicksilver**; however, this name "Mercury" is wrongly and ignorantly given to common quicksilver by those who lack knowledge. For this flowing metal is not the true Mercury, even though it consists for the most part of it. Rather, Mercury—properly speaking—is a white, transparent, mild <note>original: *unscharff*; in alchemy, this refers to a substance that is not corrosive or acidic</note>, and volatile mineral essence. It is born through self-motion and the Art from the **universal spirit-salt**, by the contracting power of the general **Vitriol** <term>Vitriol: here referring to a philosophical principle of acidity or "greenness" that fixes spirits into matter</term>. When the flow of air within the water carries the universal spirit-salt into the earth, passing through it with vapors and penetrating all **pores** <note>original Latin: *poros*</note>, the spirit-salt is drawn out and attracted from these vapors in places where **Vitriol** <note>original: *Vitriolum*</note> and **Universal Sulfur** <note>original: *Sulphur universale*</note> are found in abundance. There, it is detained and transformed into a white, transparent, mild essence, which is the true Mercury; it then moves the **Mercury** <term>represented by the symbol ☿</term>, being strengthened by the constant spirit-salt in the flowing air.

Beyond this, the Mercury is formed through the thinning of the **Salt** <note>original Latin: *Salis*</note>, and the **Vitriol**—which receives its growth from **Water** <term>represented by the symbol ▽</term>—is powerfully turned into **Mercury** <term>☿</term> through the thickening

If both **Salts** <note>original: *Salia*</note> have come together from a pure **Vehicle** <note>original: *Vehiculo*; the substance that carries the essence</note> in a good proportion of weight, and the **Air** [🜁] and the previous **Water** [🜄] from which the **Body** <note>original: *Corpus*</note> is formed are pure, they also give birth to a pure metal. If there is much **Vitriol** <term>Vitriol: a metallic sulfate, here used as a principle of fixation</term> in an impure matrix—which is the vehicle for both the vitriol and the water—then the vitriol can take in little air or **Spirit-Salt** [🜔] and turn it into **Mercury** [☿]; thus, an impure metal or mineral is produced from it. 

If, on the other hand, a pure vitriol is generated in a pure matrix where a pure flow of air is steamed through, the **Spirit-Salt** [🜔] coagulates in abundance and makes it into **Mercury** [☿]; but because of the great quantity and excessive weight, it cannot ripen or be brought to a pure, stable metal. Instead, it often remains as a **Cinnabar** <note>a bright red mineral, the primary ore of mercury</note> or **Metallic Water** [🜄] (liquid metal). In the preparation of our **Mercury** [☿] and **Gold** [☉], the fourth is always born from three things, and 1, 2, 3 go back into 1, and 1 back into 3. 

Namely, through **Salt** [🜔], **Water** [🜄], and Spirit or **Air-Salt** [🜔], **Mercury** [☿] arises; from Mercury comes **Vitriol**, and from **Water** [🜄] comes liquid metal. However, the liquid metal still lacks the right weight of vitriol; when this is replaced, it immediately becomes a metal. If it is liquid **Gold** [☉], then through the artistic addition of the universal **Gold** [☉], it becomes gold. If it is liquid **Silver** [☽], it becomes silver. If you take from it its remaining and most necessary **Salt** [🜔] by robbing it of the mixed-in **Water** [🜄], then it becomes

# Chapter XIII.

...a vapor arises from it, which—whenever it finds another **Matrix** <note>original: *Matricem*; the receptive environment or "womb" where a substance takes form</note> or merely an **Object**—settles like a white **Salt** [🜔] <note>original: *Sal*; specifically referring to the crystalline "Spirit-Salt" or Nitre</note> or white dust. From our mercury-metal, living gold, and common **Gold** [☉], our **Masterpiece** <note>original: *Meisterstück*; the *Magisterium* or the Great Work of the Alchemists</note> is made. 

I must further remind the reader of this: most people take offense at my **Human Matter** <note>original: *menschlichen Materia*; this likely refers to the use of bodily fluids like urine or blood as a starting point, a controversial "low" path in alchemy compared to using "noble" metals</note>, because my **Principle** runs contrary to almost all philosophical teachings. Those teachings generally claim that no metal can be improved except through a metallic or mineral essence, much less that the metallic **Masterpiece** can be prepared without mercury or a mercury-metallic substance. 

Therefore, they reject good advice and faithful instruction, and instead labor to find a very pure mercury-metal with which they hope to dissolve, rot <note>original: *verfaulen* / *putreficirt*; the stage of *putrefaction* where the matter turns black and "dies" before rebirth</note>, and again fix <note>original: *figiren*; to make a volatile substance solid and resistant to fire</note> common **Gold** [☉]. Others hold with **Antimony** [♁] and seek it out through various ways. They believe that the **Mercury** [☿] or **Antimony** [♁] of the ancients was not a metal or mineral at all, but a penetrating **Salt** [🜔] <note>likely referring to Saltpeter or Nitre</note> with which their **Antimony** [♁] must be unlocked, putrefied, and fixed into a **Tincture** <term>Tincture: a potent alchemical liquid capable of transmuting base metals or healing the body</term>. 

Yet, I do not wish to refute anyone here, nor take the trouble to show everyone how greatly they have erred in the choice of their **Antimonies** [♁], **Metals**, and **Salts** [🜔]. It is impossible for a pure **Antimony** [♁] or a purer **Salt** [🜔] for our **Magistery** <note>the completed alchemical work</note> to be made from the **Earth** [🜃] that is under our feet. ### On Mercury, Vitriol, and Sulfur.

**M**

Spirit or **Air-Salt** <note>original: *Lufft-Salz*; a volatile salt alchemists believed could be captured from the atmosphere, often associated with the "World Spirit"</note> of **Mercury** [☿] is so firmly united with **Vitriol** [🜖] <note>original: *Vitriolo*; a category of metal sulfates used as powerful acidic reagents</note> that—aside from the fact that much impurity (which cannot be separated during the dissolving movement without losing the best and purest parts of the **Spirit-Salt** <note>original: *Geist-Saltz*; the essential, refined salt-essence of a substance</note> or the Mercury) arises in the union of these two general Salts—it is impossible to pull them apart again and strip the **Vitriol** of its **Mercury** without adding general, pure **Mercury** or **Spirit-Salt**. Even if one finally obtains the desired **Mercury** [☿] in a pure state and separated from all "bad nature" <note>original: *Unart*; referring to the impurities or "base" qualities that hinder alchemical transformation</note>, it is still "meager" <note>original: *mager*; lacking the "fatness" or sulfurous richness required for the Work</note>. This process requires the general Mercury, of which a mere ten grains are far more precious than a whole pound of Mercury produced in the manner described above. Thus, it is not worth the effort—it would be like "throwing a gemstone to crack a nut."

If one already possesses the **Universal and Undetermined Mercury** <note>original: *universalem und undeterminierten Mercurium*; the primal, raw form of Mercury that has not yet been "determined" into a specific metal like lead or gold</note>, it is unnecessary and foolish to use it simply to obtain a lesser substance. If one does not have the **Universal**, then the labor of separating a lesser mercury from metals, minerals, or salts—in the essence, power, and purity necessary for the **Metallic Masterpiece** <note>original: *Metallischen Meisterstück*; the *Magisterium* or the ultimate goal of the alchemical process</note>—is in vain. I can indeed, with little expense, make mercury-metals and even **Mercury** [☿] from various metals and minerals without the immediate help of the **Spirit-Salt** or the **Universal Mercury**; but this **Solution** <note>original: *Solution*; the process of dissolving a solid into a liquid state</note> and **Separation** <note>original: *Separation*; the alchemical stage of isolating the different components (Salt, Sulfur, Mercury) of a substance</note> are of no use, and they yield such

# Chapter XIII.
<meta>catchword: Gese-</meta>

**Mercury-metal** <note>original: *Queck-Metall*; liquid metallic mercury</note> offers only *specific* <note>original: *particulare*; referring to effects on individual metals rather than the "Universal" work of transformation</note> advantages, because too much impurity <note>original: *Unart*; literally "bad behavior" or "bad nature," referring to chemical impurities that hinder the process</note> always remains in its **vitriol**. Therefore, in comparison to the **universal vitriol**, it accomplishes little in the ripening of other metals, and even then, only very rarely.

My **Human Matter** <note>original: *Menschliche Materia*; a common alchemical code for urine or other bodily fluids, believed by many "chymists" of the 17th century to contain the concentrated essence of the World Spirit</note> does not only manage to draw the **Universal Spirit-Salt** from the air and transform it into a pure **Mercury** and mercury-metal through its pure vitriol; this work also proceeds from beginning to end without money and without great effort. It costs nothing but a little time, for anyone who wishes to perform it in silence.

Why then will people be so stubborn and refuse to be shown the way and set right by a faithful teacher? For I testify publicly that my **Human Matter** produces the general **Mercury** from the general **Spirit-Salt** of the air without the addition of any other thing. Have I not—like others—sufficient years to teach? (Since **GOD** the **LORD**, whom I thank again most humbly, discovered this great secret to me in the 23rd year of my age, and I have now only just passed my thirtieth year). Thus, I have gained much experience and have come so far in the knowledge of **Self-Movement** <note>original: *Selbst-Bewegung*; the alchemical concept of a substance that can react or transform on its own without external heat or reagents</note> that I am able to help others, and I wish to help gladly. Were it not for the simple love of my neighbor, truly, I would not set pen to paper to write, because my...

# Of Mercury, Vitriol, and Sulfur <note>original: *Vom Merc. Vitriolo und Schweffel*; Mercury, Vitriol, and Sulfur were the three primary principles of alchemy, representing spirit, soul, and body</note>

Circumstances and understanding are so arranged that the greatest fame—knowing much in such matters—cannot provide me with the slightest advantage nor even a shadow of honor. Yet, it pleases me nonetheless when my scattered seed grows in the world and brings forth fruit; as various pious hearts have reported to me, to my particular pleasure, that **GOD** is richly blessing them upon the path I have shown.

What more heartfelt, powerful, and pure **Mercury** <note>original: *Mercurium*; refers here to the "Universal Spirit" or the "living" metallic essence</note> could you desire for the art of metals <note>original: *Metallen-Kunst*; alchemy</note> than that which is born from **Heaven** and a **King**—namely, yourself? Born from **Heaven**, which gives life to all things and sustains everything; and from **You**, the lower king of the world, who rules over all things and enjoys all things. 

Must not such a **Mercury** necessarily be better than the one that has been, so to speak, thrust from its throne, the aerial heaven, and fettered in the prison of the earth? From there, no art or force will release it so well or so soon that it has not first lost the fat from its body and the marrow from its bones in those hard bonds and in the labor of resolution <note>original: *Auflösung*; the chemical process of breaking down a solid into a liquid or gas</note>. In contrast, **Heaven** and **Man** give birth to a **Royal Prince**, and leave the true artist in full health and vitality—

# Chapter XIII.
<note>original: *Caput XIII*</note>

...deliver it to life, so that he may raise it according to his wisdom, crown it with gold at the appointed time, and place it in full command of the Metallic and Mineral Kingdom.

This is the Mighty One, praised by Hermes in the **Emerald Tablet** <note>The *Tabula Smaragdina*, a foundational cryptic text of alchemy attributed to Hermes Trismegistus</note>: He, the true living **Mercury** <note>original: *Mercurius*</note>, is not called living simply because he becomes a liquid metal—for that is merely fluid and not truly alive—but rather because in him alone resides the living seed of Gold. Though it is small in weight, it is so great in power that even a small amount can bring ten to twelve times its weight in common fine gold to life and subtly expand it.

The <term>Mercurial Glue</term> <note>original: *Mercurial-Leim*; a sticky, intermediate substance in the alchemical process thought to join different elements</note>—if the living gold had not been born within it through its own <term>Vitriol</term> <note>original: *Vitriol*; usually referring to metallic sulfates, but here used for a potent, acidic "green" solvent or essence</note> before being set by the artist—could not make common gold living and fruitful. Instead, the center of **Mercury**, which is a grainy, living gold and pure <term>Tincture</term> <note>original: *Tinctur*; the concentrated essence used to transmute base metals into gold</note>, enters the gold once the gold has been unlocked <note>original: *aufgeschlossen*; a term for dissolving or breaking down a substance to reveal its inner nature</note> by the **Mercury**. It then adopts its now living-grainy form and multiplies itself hundreds—and, depending on the labor, thousands—of times through nourishment from the **Mercury** and the water within the **Vitriol**.
